## Current Objective

Build the durable shell/foundation before further content work. The accepted design canon uses a clear reference hierarchy:

- MN is the primary structural, template-geometry, spacing, responsive, and mobile-layout reference.
- AMS is the polished visual reference for typography finish, controls, modal presentation, compactness, and general visual refinement.
- Preserve each book's accent color and subject identity. Use original ATI template layouts only for official structural labels and box relationships, never as permission to add content.
- When a difference is not already resolved by canon, stop for focused comparison rather than inventing a broad hybrid rule.
- Use Wide desktop overlay only when MN and Pediatric share one full available-width canvas, capped at `1440px`; use Split-pane only for responsive checks. An active overlay MUST mirror the MN scroll position immediately and MUST NOT be judged from a half-width responsive iframe.
- For same-role sidebar vertical parity, compare the rendered header, theme control, progress strip, search panel, search input, section label, and every template group at desktop, tablet, and phone widths. MN's accepted stack uses a `29px` theme control, `33.5px` search input, and `8px 0 24px` list padding; same-role geometry MUST remain within `1px`.

Completed automation objectives: private `Elevated_ATI_Build_Guide_v4_AI_Spec.md` is a self-contained, cold-start-validated AI execution specification with 1617 unique normative requirements, and the complete MN/MH/AMS template clipping matrix has passed final cross-book integrity. The user completed and approved the overall MN/MH/AMS template visual review on 2026-07-17. Those two automations remain paused. Pediatric Batches 0-10 remain valid structure, behavior, and clipping evidence, but their ready-for-review state is superseded as current status by Part 115 because user review confirmed typography and search-treatment mismatches.

## Accepted Template Canon Snapshot

- Main template landing pages should use MN's spacing rhythm, typography scale, search panel treatment, progress rows, footer/reset treatment, and measured landmark alignment. At desktop and split-pane widths, each collapsed template accordion renders at 92px outer height with a 90px header, 18px 22px header padding, 14px flex gap, and an 8px progress track. Keep the header controls as direct flex children in this order: caret, title, A-page badge, row print button, studied count, progress track. This direct order preserves MN's badge and print-control placement across pane widths.
- Opened template modals should keep the larger AMS-style title treatment while using MN's readable body text, calmer controls, source-line structure, box spacing, and mobile stacking behavior.
- Every worked template card should render its empty Student Name value as the shared AMS/MH-style writing line: at least `120px` wide, `18px` high, and a `1px dashed` current-border-color bottom edge in both Filled and Blank modes. The line must remain inside its metadata value cell at desktop, split-pane/tablet, and phone widths.
- Across Peds A1-A15, each `dl.template-meta` must end with an `18px` boundary before its first primary content group and primary box grids must use a `14px` relationship gap. Preserve intentional local internals: A5 Growth and Development uses its `20px` outer / `12px` child rhythm, and A7 Medication Purpose uses its `10px` inset / `10px` inner gap. Do not use a shared primitive to flatten those accepted structures.
- For A1/A3/A5/A7/A9 typography, use MN A7 Medication's `Complications` box as the readable body-text canon across books: actual `p`, `li`, and direct body text in both main and nested boxes uses 16px DM Sans at 1.6 line-height with normal weight. Keep existing MN heading-strip typography unchanged. Validate rendered child text rather than only the parent `.content`, because child rules can silently restore smaller text.
- `Open chapter` links belong on their own line below the source chapter/title line.
- Internal source labels such as `ALS in book` should not appear in learner-facing source lines.
- A5 Growth and Development should preserve MN's outer section card, inner boxes, connector behavior, and progress bar. The Expected Growth and Development divider and child boxes stay inside the bordered, padded outer group; only the Health Promotion tree keeps a transparent outer wrapper for its connectors. Wide-grid siblings remain equal through normal row stretching, while each row is sized by its tallest real content; do not impose fixed `260px`/`300px` minimum heights. At split-pane and mobile widths, stacked A5 boxes use natural content height so sparse books do not retain large empty regions.
- A7 Medication should preserve the official MN/ATI medication skeleton, including Purpose of Medication, official left/right body columns, and the Nursing Interventions to Client Education connector. The Purpose group uses a `10px` inset and `10px` inner gap so its two equal official boxes share Pediatric's approved wider geometry at the same outer width. Its two regular inner Purpose headers use the Pediatric-approved `15px` type, `10px 16px` padding, `17.25px` line height, and `38.25px` rendered height inside their existing `11px` frames; do not let the generic compact header override replace this stack. Direct body title strips use the MN-rendered `47px` stack: `11px 16px` padding, `24px` line height, and existing `13px` top caps; do not let the generic compact `10px` header override shorten them.
- A7 Medication metadata labels MUST use DM Sans `10px`, `700` weight, `16px` line height, and `1.4px` letter spacing. The four labels remain part of the existing metadata structure; do not add, remove, rename, or reorder them to satisfy a typography check.
- A9 Nursing Skill should preserve the official six-box skeleton and keep source sections routed into the correct boxes instead of removing empty official slots. The two nested Considerations boxes use the same 16px DM Sans, 1.6 line-height body typography as the direct A9 boxes. A9 uses book-accent bullets, muted body text, and the book's solid primary text color for bold emphasis, including `Pre:`, `Intra:`, and `Post:`. Match MN's rounded A9 corner family exactly: 16px for the Considerations group, 14px for direct boxes with 13px header caps, and 11px for nested boxes with 10px header caps. At MN's wide and split-pane sizes, preserve the two-column procedure grid; collapse to one column only at the MN phone breakpoint of 680px. A9-specific grid-area rules must agree with the active column count so no implicit 2px column or oversized Considerations row can form.
- A11 System Disorder should preserve the official skeleton. On wide screens, Safety Considerations and Complications stay in one right rail with equal content-aware rows that grow to the larger content requirement; do not use shrinkable fractional rows or forced full-height children that can clip text. At narrow/tablet widths, explicitly override A11's higher-specificity desktop tracks so both the outer grid and filled top row use one column; never combine a one-column area map with two-column tracks. The Safety rail returns to natural vertical stacking, Assessment remains a 2-by-2 inner grid, and Patient-Centered Care uses the stacked five-box layout for readability.
- A13 Therapeutic Procedure follows the shared procedure-family sizing rules. Above its accepted 900px safeguard, paired rows stay equal but grow to the larger real content requirement; direct Considerations subboxes must not shrink or clip. At 900px and below, use one full-width MN-style vertical stack with natural-height Considerations boxes and hide the horizontal connector.
- A15 Concept Analysis preserves the simple official skeleton: two metadata rows (`Student Name` and `Concept Analysis`), Defining Characteristics spanning both wide columns, then paired Antecedents/Negative Consequences and Related Concepts/Exemplars. Use the shared 14px box gap and corners, 16px/1.6 body typography, natural content heights, and A15's own approximately 720px one-column breakpoint.
- Low-priority controls such as `Reset All Progress` should use MN's quiet text-button treatment: transparent background, inherited font, 11px size, and normal weight.

## Rollout Rules

- Do not roll out to all books in one run.
- Work one book template file at a time.
- Within each book, work one template family or one visual defect per batch.
- Preserve each book's accent color, subject identity, template counts, and existing educational content.
- Do not add templates, flashcards, prompts, cards, sections, enrichment, or ATI educational content while content population is paused.
- Use recovered screenshots only as QA/reference context, not public content.
- Before editing, state the exact file and layout area. Validate with static checks, script parsing, and visual comparison after editing.
- Never mark a visual task approved without user confirmation.
- Never stage, commit, push, merge, reset, revert, or delete without the user's explicit instruction.

## Documentation Continuity Protocol

- After every completed batch, update the private master Build Guide and this living overlay with the active book/template, completed work, files involved, validation, visual-approval status, unresolved issue, Git state, and exactly one next action.
- Check every current guide after each batch and update every affected guide so the current documents cannot contradict one another.
- Update `MN Design Canon Checklist.md` whenever an accepted canon rule is added, changed, clarified, or rejected.
- Update `AMS Prototype MN Canon Audit.md` whenever AMS audit status, comparison evidence, or rollout readiness changes.
- Keep the archived July 4 branch immutable as the recovery baseline.
- No migration-critical decision, pending approval, active task, or next action may exist only in chat history.
- Before ending a batch, confirm that a new task could resume safely from the private master, this overlay, the canon checklist, and the Git diff.

## Required Project Response Format

Every substantive project-work closeout or status update must use exactly these fields in this order, with one blank line between them: `Current mode:`, `Current batch:`, `Files edited:`, `Validation performed:`, `Automation status:`, `Commit/push status:`, `GitHub Desktop should show:`, `TLDR:`, and `Next Recommended Action:`.

- State whether content population is paused or active and include `ATI source support used: none; design-only change.` for design-only work.
- State that `ati-build-guide-ai-spec-polish`, `ati-template-clipping-qa`, and `ati-pediatric-templates-design-polish` remain paused and unchanged.
- Use `Files edited: none.` when applicable and clearly state whether the user needs to commit or push.
- Mention `.DS_Store`, `Teleprompter.html`, and `recovered-codex-screenshots/` only under `GitHub Desktop should show:`.
- Keep `TLDR:` concise. End with exactly one next recommended action and no prose after it.
- The action must plainly say whether the user personally needs to do anything, without separate AI/manual categories. Avoid long technical dumps unless needed for a blocker or validation result.

## Current Rollout Status

AMS and MN Templates have completed their major design-canon alignment. Their accepted comparison work established:

- main template landing-page alignment;
- source-line and modal header rhythm;
- quiet text/control token matching;
- A5 Growth and Development mobile/card behavior;
- A7 Medication structure and connector behavior;
- A9 Nursing Skill official-box preservation;
- A11 System Disorder Assessment and stacked Patient-Centered Care behavior;
- A13 procedure-family narrow/mobile behavior;
- A15 visual-token consistency.

MN has been changed only when explicitly approved during this prototype work, such as adding Previous/Next navigation to MN template modals, applying the stacked five-box Patient-Centered Care narrow layout, and adding the shared AMS/MH-style dashed Student Name writing line to every MN worked template card.

The MH Templates focused rollout is complete. The landing shell, sidebar controls, typography, accordion geometry, A-page badges, print controls, A3/A5/A7/A9 structures, and A9 corner/emphasis canon were aligned in small visual batches. MH A11 System Disorder now explicitly stacks the outer grid, filled top row, Safety rail, and five Patient-Centered Care boxes at narrow/tablet widths while preserving the 2-by-2 Assessment grid. MH A13 Therapeutic Procedure now replaces its higher-specificity desktop tracks with one full-width column and natural-height Considerations boxes at narrow/tablet widths. MH A15 Concept Analysis now uses MN's shared visual tokens, natural heights, official two-row metadata, and two-column-to-one-column responsive map. The final shell pass added MN's `max-width:1100px` main-workspace padding to MH and objectively validated the landing shell, sidebar, accordion, modal, navigation, count, clipping, search, and responsive behavior.

The MN A13 repair reported after that queue now passes objective clipping QA. Its content-aware procedure rows preserve equal left/right sizing while allowing the taller real content to determine row height. The user completed and approved the overall template visual review on 2026-07-17.

The Pediatric Nursing Templates family, structure, behavior, clipping, section-first visual parity, and final cross-surface handoff are passed. Parts 130-179 preserve the completed section and family review evidence; Part 180 closes Section 6 and supersedes every earlier Pediatric active-item statement. The Pediatric automation remains paused, and no Pharmacology batch is active.

## Comparative Mode Design Contract — Canonical Requirement

This contract governs every future Elevated ATI visual-comparison handoff unless the latest explicit user instruction changes it.

1. **MN is the fixed left shared-canon reference.** Every design-canon comparison must place the exact finalized MN product in the clearly labeled left pane unless the user explicitly directs otherwise. A matching finalized MH or AMS family may be used only when the user or established family evidence specifically requires it; it must never silently replace MN.
2. **Exactly two panes are visible at a time, in fixed order.** The left pane is MN reference; the right pane is the current target. A multi-target, cross-book, or final review uses a pair selector or sequential two-pane links that change only the right current-file pane. It must never reverse the MN/current order or display a third simultaneous comparison pane unless the user explicitly requests that change.
3. **The established featureful dark shell is preserved in a compact disclosure.** The header keeps the loading/ready/error status visible and places the full control set inside a clearly labeled `Comparison options` disclosure that is collapsed by default. Expanding the disclosure overlays the comparison instead of permanently increasing header height or shrinking the panes. Required controls remain synchronized Filled/Blank; a pair selector that changes only the right target when more than one target is in scope; Align All to Top; family landmark alignment controls; Sync Once; proportional continuous Sync Scroll; horizontal Guides; linked movable Vertical Guides; persistent pane/book/card labels; and full-height equal panes. Controls may be added when explicitly requested, but must not be removed, renamed in a way that loses their purpose, or replaced by a reduced shell without explicit user approval.
4. **Comparison inputs and roles are exact.** Each pane must load a fresh, byte-verified product payload through a unique cache-busted `127.0.0.1` URL: finalized MN on the left and exact-current target on the right. The shell must open the intended family/card and map equivalent semantic roles without changing learner text, product files, accents, or subject identity.
5. **The shell is itself a gated artifact.** Before handoff, verify the expected pane count/order/labels, exact payload mapping, collapsed-default options state, visible status, compact header geometry, expanded overlay geometry without pane reflow, every control inside the disclosure, Filled and Blank synchronization across every pane, top and landmark alignment, one-time and continuous scroll synchronization, horizontal and linked vertical guides, loaded fonts, stable ready state, equal visible panes, and HTTP success. Inspect fresh collapsed and expanded screenshots. Automated checks are evidence only and never user visual approval.
6. **No silent comparison regression.** Requested comparison features and orientation are persistent workflow requirements, not disposable temporary QA scaffolding. Never increase or reduce the canonical two-pane display, move MN away from the left, omit MN, restyle the established shell, remove a requested feature, or substitute a different reference because a later checkpoint says only “comparison” or “handoff.” Any proposed contract change is subjective and requires explicit user approval first.
